    NOTES TO THE UNAUDITED INTERIM CONDENSED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS



    1.   一般資料                         1. GENERAL INFORMATION
    大新金融集團有限公司（ 「本公司」
                    ）與其附屬             Dah Sing Financial Holdings Limited (the “Company”) and its
    公司（統稱
        「本集團」）在香港、澳門及中國提供             subsidiaries (together the “Group”) provide banking, insurance,
    銀行、保險、金融及其他相關服務。                  financial and other related services in Hong Kong, Macau, and the
                                      Peopleʼs Republic of China.

    2.   未經審核之財務報表及會計政策               2. U N A U D I T E D F I N A N C I A L S TAT E M E N T S A N D
                                         ACCOUNTING POLICIES
    載於本中期業績報告之資料不構成法定                 The information set out in this Interim Report does not constitute
    之綜合財務報表。                          statutory consolidated financial statements.

    本 中 期 業 績 報 告 之 若 干 財 務 資 料，乃 摘   Certain financial information in this Interim Report is extracted
    錄自根據香港公司條例第622章第662(3)            from the statutory consolidated financial statements for the
    條及附表6第3部分已送呈公司註冊處及                year ended 31 December 2023 (the “2023 consolidated financial
    香港金融管理局（「香港金管局」）之截至               statements”) which have been delivered to the Registrar of
    2023年12月31日止年度之法定綜合財務報            Companies as required by section 662(3) of, and Part 3 of Schedule
    表（「2023年綜合財務報表」      ）。           6 to, the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance (Cap.622), and the

                                      (Continued)

聯營公司投資之減值虧損（續）                    Impairment loss on investment in an associate (Continued)

減值測試是以投資的賬面值與由計算使用價               The impairment test is performed by comparing the carrying
值（
 「使用價值」）而釐定之重慶銀行可收回金              amount of the investment against the higher of the recoverable
額及該投資之公平值當中較高者作出對比。               amount of BOCQ, determined by a value in use (“VIU”) calculation,
使用價值乃按照管理層估量之重慶銀行盈利               and fair value of the investment. The VIU calculation uses
和未來將派股息，及經考慮重慶銀行中期和               discounted cash flow projections based on managementʼs
長期之增長及資產淨值後之預期未來的可能               estimates of BOCQʼs earnings and dividends to be paid in future,
脫手價值，應用貼現現金流量預測計算。應用              and the estimated probable exit value in future after considering
於使用價值計算之貼現率乃參考可於公開市               the growth of BOCQ and its net asset value for the medium and
場獲取之重慶銀行股本成本而估算。                  longer term. The discount rate applied to the VIU calculation was
                                  estimated with reference to BOCQʼs cost of equity, which is publicly
                                  available in the market.

進行使用價值計算以得出該投資之可收回金               In performing the VIU calculation to arrive at the recoverable
額時，本集團考慮所有相關因素包括市場觀               amount of the investment, the Group considers all relevant factors
點及質化因素以確保計算使用價值之參數合               including market views and qualitative factors to ensure that the
適。調整亦須作出以反映影響重慶銀行之最               inputs to the VIU calculation are appropriate. Adjustments need
新情況及對預測重慶銀行未來表現有關之中               to be made to reflect the latest situation affecting BOCQ and also
期及長期市場展望。在估算重慶銀行之未來               market outlook for the medium and longer term that are relevant
現金流當中需要管理層作重要判斷。                  in projecting BOCQʼs future performance. Significant management
                                  judgement is required in estimating the future cash flows of BOCQ.

就2024年6月30日之狀況，基於12.0%（ 2023年     For the position as at 30 June 2024, the recoverable amount
12月31日： 12.0%）貼現率計算使用價值所進         concluded by the latest impairment test performed based on
行最新減值測試推斷出的可回收金額，及投               the VIU calculation using a discount rate of 12.0% (31 December
資之公平值，皆被評估為比扣除截至2023年             2023: 12.0%) and the fair value of the investment are assessed as
12月31日之累計減值計提5,227,000,000港元後     lower than the carrying amount, after deducting the cumulative
之賬面值分別低出85,000,000港元及16,000,000   impairment allowance made up to 31 December 2023 of HK$5,227

     11. 稅項                           11. TAXATION
     香港利得稅乃按照期內估計應課稅溢利以稅              Hong Kong profits tax has been provided at the rate of 16.5%
     率16.5%（ 2023年：16.5%）提撥準備。中國內地    (2023: 16.5%) on the estimated assessable profit for the period.
     及澳門稅款乃按期內估計應課稅溢利依本集              Taxation on profits in Mainland China and Macau has been
     團經營業務地區之現行稅率計算。                  calculated on the estimated assessable profit for the period at the
                                      rates of taxation prevailing in the countries in which the Group
                                      operates.

     遞延稅項是採用負債法就暫時差異，按預期              Deferred taxation is calculated in full on temporary differences
     該等稅項負債需清付時或資產可予扣減時所              under the liability method at the tax rates that are expected to
     適用之稅率作全數確認。                      apply in the year when the liability is settled or the asset is realised.

30. 營業分項報告                    30. OPERATING SEGMENT REPORTING
本集團根據香港財務報告準則第8號《營運業          Segment reporting by the Group is prepared in accordance with
務分項》編製分項報告。向包括行政總裁及其          HKFRS 8 “Operating Segments”. Information reported to the
他執行委員會成員之主要營運決策人呈報而           chief operating decision maker, including the Chief Executive
作為資源分配及業績評估用途之資料，乃按           and other Executive Committee members, for the purposes of
銀行業務及保險業務之基礎來確定。本地銀           resource allocation and performance assessment, is determined
行業務之營運表現按業務活動分析，而中國           on the basis of banking business and insurance business. For
內地及澳門之銀行業務之營運表現按業務機           banking business, operating performances are analysed by
構分析。對於保險業，資源配置和表現評價是          business activities for local banking business, and on business
基於保險的企業實體的基礎。                 entity basis for banking businesses in Mainland China and Macau.
                              For insurance business, resources allocation and performance
                              evaluation are based on insurance business entity basis.

經考慮到本地業務之客戶群、產品及服務，經          Considering the customer groups, products and services of local
濟環境和法規後，本集團將營運業務劃分為           businesses, the economic environment and regulations, the Group
下列呈報分項：                       splits the operating segments of the Group into the following
                              reportable segments:

•   個人銀行業務包括接受個人客戶存款、         •   Personal banking business includes the acceptance of
    住宅樓宇按揭、私人貸款、透支、汽車貸            deposits from individual customers and the extension of
    款和信用卡服務、保險業務的銷售和投             residential mortgage lending, personal loans, overdraft,
    資服務。                          vehicle financing and credit card services, and the provision of
                                  insurance sales and investment services.

•   企業銀行業務包括接受存款、貸款、營運        •   Corporate banking business includes the acceptance of
    資金融資及貿易融資，其存款來源及融             deposits from and the advance of loans and working capital
    資客戶主要是工商業及機構性客戶。              finance to commercial, industrial and institutional customers,
                                  and the provision of trade financing.

•   財資及環球市場業務主要包括外匯服          •   Treasury and global markets activities are mainly the
    務、中央貸存現金管理、利率風險管理、            provision of foreign exchange services and centralised cash
    證券投資管理及本集團整體之資金運用             management for deposit taking and lending, interest rate risk

     就編製分項報告而言，對可直接認明為各個              For the purpose of segment reporting, revenue derived from
     別分項之源自客戶、產品及服務收入，將直接             customers, products and services directly identifiable with
     呈報於有關分項；而分項之間的資金運用及              individual segments are reported directly under respective
     資金資源所產生的收入和資金成本，按參照              segments, while revenue and funding cost arising from inter-
     市場利率之轉移價格機制分配至各分項。分              segment funding operation and funding resources are allocated
     項間之交易乃依據授予第三者或與第三者交              to segments by way of transfer pricing mechanism with reference
     易之同類條款定價。分項間之收入或支出於              to market interest rates. Transactions within segments are priced
     綜合賬內抵銷。                          based on similar terms offered to or transacted with external
                                      parties. Inter-segment income or expenses are eliminated on
                                      consolidation.

     所有不同分項之直接開支將歸類於有關的分              All direct costs incurred by different segments are grouped under
     項分類。間接開支及支援部門開支乃依據開              respective segments. Indirect costs and support functionsʼ costs
     支性質，按耗用之時間及工作量和分項營運              are allocated to various segments and products based on effort
     收入，分配至不同的分項及產品。不能合理地             and time spent as well as segmentsʼ operating income depending
     分配至各分項、產品及支援部門之企業活動              on the nature of costs incurred. Costs related to corporate activities
     開支，則作企業開支呈列於
                「其他」項下。               that cannot be reasonably allocated to segments, products and
                                      support functions are grouped under Others as unallocated
                                      corporate expenses.

     33. 風險管理                         33. RISK MANAGEMENT
     甲. 集團銀行系                         A.   Banking Group

     本集團明瞭各類風險會不停蛻變的特性並透              The Group recognises the changing nature of risk and manages it
     過完善的管理架構作有效管理。                   through a well-developed management structure.

     風險管理專注於信貸風險、市場風險、利率風             Risk management is focused on credit risk, market risk, interest
     險、流動資金風險、操作風險、聲譽風險及策             rate risk, liquidity risk, operational risk, reputation risk and
     略性風險。信貸風險之產生主要源於本集團              strategic risk. Credit risk occurs mainly in the Groupʼs credit
     之信貸組合，其中包括企業和零售借貸、機械             portfolios comprising corporate and retail lending, equipment and
     和租購融資及財資和金融機構的批發借貸及              hire purchase financing, and treasury and financial institutions
     財資投資。                            wholesale lending and treasury investment.

     大部份的市場風險乃源於財資及環球市場部              Market risk arises mainly from Treasury & Global Markets Division
     （「財資部」），主要與本集團資產負債表內             (“TRD”) and is associated principally with the Groupʼs on- and off-
     及資產負債表外之買賣交易及其證券投資有              balance sheet positions in the trading book and its investment
     關。                               securities.

     利率風險指因利率的不利變動而引致本集團              Interest rate risk means the risk to the Groupʼs financial condition
     的財政狀況面臨的風險。                      resulting from adverse movements in interest rates.

     流動資金風險乃指本集團未能在不衍生不可              Liquidity risk is the risk that the Group is unable to fund
     接受損失的情況下為新增的資產融資或就到              increases in assets or meet its payment obligations associated
     期之金融負債履行付款責任。                    with its financial liabilities when they fall due without incurring
                                      unacceptable losses.

     操作風險乃因內部程序、員工及系統之不足              Operational risk is the risk of loss (direct or indirect) resulting from
     與疏忽或外來的事件而產生之直接或間接虧              inadequate or failed internal processes, people and systems or
     損之風險。                            from external events.

     聲譽風險是指由於本集團之商業慣例、營運              Reputation risk is the risk arising from the potential that negative
     誤差或營運表現而可能帶來之負面宣傳風               publicity regarding the Groupʼs business practices, operational
     險。這些負面因素不論是否屬實，均可能令              errors or operating performance, whether true or not, could cause
     客戶產生憂慮或負面看法，削弱客戶基礎及              customer concerns or negative view, decline in the customer base
     市場佔有率或導致耗費龐大之訴訟或減少收              or market share, or lead to costly litigation or revenue reductions.
     入。

     策略性風險泛指由於差劣之策略性決定、不              Strategic risk generally refers to the corporate risk that may bring
     可接受之財務表現、策略施行上失當以及對              significant immediate or future negative impact on the financial
     於市場轉變欠缺有效應變而可能對集團財務              and market positions of the Group because of poor strategic
     及市場狀況有即時或日後重大負面影響之企              decisions, unacceptable financial performance, and inappropriate
     業風險。                             implementation of strategies and lack of effective response to the
                                      market changes.

33. 風險管理（續）                    33. RISK MANAGEMENT (Continued)
甲. 集團銀行系（續）                    A.   Banking Group (Continued)

其他方面之風險管理詳述如下。                 The other risk management aspects are disclosed below.

  （甲）集團風險管理架構                       (a) The risk management structure of the Group

  董事會對所有類別的風險管理負上整體                 The Board of Directors has the broad overall responsibility for
  的責任。關於風險控制方面，董事會的責                the management of all types of risk. The responsibilities of the

  本集團接受定息或浮息及不同年期之客                 The Group accepts deposits from customers at both fixed
  戶存款，並以此取得之資金投資於各種                 and floating rates, and for various periods, and seeks to earn

  時並保持足夠之流動資金以應付可能須                 longer periods at higher rates, while maintaining sufficient
  付之所有到期債務。                         liquidity to meet all claims that might fall due.

  本集團亦按信貸風險及市場情況，透過                 The Group also seeks to apply its interest margins through
  向企業及零售借款人貸款賺取息差，以                 its lending to corporate and retail borrowers and to charge
  及向客戶收取合理費用及佣金。此等活                 customers appropriate fees and commission, taking into
  動風險不單涉及資產負債表內之貸款及                 consideration credit risk and market conditions. Such

  擔，例如信用證、履約保證及其他保證。                advances, as the Group also enters into guarantees and other

     截至2024年6月30日止6個月，美國較                In the six months to 30 June 2024, higher inflation rates
     高的通脹率引致預期的減息延遲。                     in the US had led to delays in the expected reduction
     持續的高利率和較高的地緣政治                      in interest rates. The persistent high interest rates
     風險預計會壓抑借貸和投資活動。                     and heightened geopolitical risks were expected to
     儘管近期有政策的支援，但這些外                     dampen borrowing and investment activities. These
     部壓力和金融限制導致整體前景略                     external pressures and financial constraints led to a
     為悲觀。另一方面，中國內地房地                     slightly pessimistic overall outlook despite recent policy
     產開發商信貸情況惡化所帶來的影                     efforts. On the other hand, the impact brought by the
     響，在2024年上半年仍然對信貸組                   credit deterioration of PRC property developers sector
     合的資產質素管理構成挑戰。在這                     remained as a challenge in managing asset quality of
     種背景下，本集團繼續採取適當的                     the credit portfolios in the first half of 2024. Against this
     評估和風險管理程序來計量預期信                     background, the Group continued to adopt appropriate
     貸虧損，包括審閱信貸組合、考慮                     assessment and risk management procedures in
     不同的宏觀經濟預測情境、預期信                     the measurement of ECL, including reviews of credit
     貸虧損模型計量結果的合理性，及                     portfolios, considering different macroeconomic forecast
     預期信貸虧損撥備的充足性。                       scenarios, as well as assessing reasonableness of ECL
                                         models outputs, and adequacy of ECL allowances.

              的風險敞口金額為投資組合的                         overlay was 0.17% of the portfolio. With the end of
              0.17%。隨著預先批核還息不還                      the PPPHS in October 2023, the Group had been
              本計劃於2023年10月結束，本                      monitoring the performance of the exposures
              集團持續監測已進行了管理層                         subject to management overlay to assess whether is
              疊加的風險敞口的表現，以評                         still necessary. In light of the stabilized credit quality
              估管理層疊加是否仍然合適。                         of the relevant exposures, the Group had removed
              鑒於相關風險敞口的信貸質素                         the related overlay amount in the first half of 2024.

     易持倉中存在市場風險。                            securities, equity securities and derivatives.

           (1) 市場風險計量方法                           (1) Market risk measurement technique

           作為市場風險管理，本集團使                          In the management of market risk, the Group
           用各種業界普遍採用之方法計                          measures market risks using various techniques
           量市場風險及控制市場風險於                          commonly used by the industry and control market
           設定之風險額度範圍內。主要                          risk exposures within established risk limits. The
           用於計量及監控市場風險之計                          major measurement techniques used to measure
           量方法概述如下。                               and control market risk are outlined below.

              •   市場風險數值                              •    Value-at-risk

              本集團應用市場風險數值                             The Group applies a VaR methodology, which
              法，其乃一種以統計為基                             is a statistically based estimate, to measure
              準就持作交易用途組合因                             the potential loss of its trading portfolio from
              市場不利變化預計潛在損                             adverse market movements. It expresses as
              失。其表明本集團於某個                             the maximum amount the Group might lose
              置信水平可能損失之最大                             given a certain level of confidence, which
              數額，就一日持倉期之基                             for the Group is 99% for a one day holding
              準作推算，本集團之置信                             period. There is therefore a specified statistical
              水平為99%。因此存在明                            probability that actual loss could be greater
              確的統計概率，實際損失                             than the VaR estimate. Hence, the use of VaR
              可能比市場風險數值之估                             does not prevent losses outside the VaR limits
              計數為大。因此採納該方                             in the event of extreme market movements.
              法並不能避免當市場狀況
              發生重大變化時超逾此等
              額度之損失。

              市場風險數值模型假設某                             The VaR model assumes a certain “holding
              個持倉期（就本集團而言                             period” (one day in the case of the Group)
              為一天）
                 直至結束持倉。市                             until positions can be closed. It is calculated
              場風險數值亦依據持倉之                             based on the current mark-to-market value
              現時市值、市場風險因素                             of the positions, the historical correlation
              過往在一個曆年週期之相                             and volatilities of the market risk factors over
              互關係及波幅，使用一種                             an observation period of one calendar year
              歷史模擬方法計算。                               using a method known as historical simulation
                                                      approach.

  （庚）流動資金風險                         (g) Liquidity risk

  流動資金風險乃指本集團未能在不衍                  Liquidity risk is the risk that the Group is unable to fund
  生不可接受之損失的情況下為新增的資                 increases in assets or meet its payment obligations associated
  產融資或就到期之金融負債履行付款責                 with its financial liabilities when they fall due without
  任。                                incurring unacceptable loss.

  本集團按審慎原則管理資金流動性，旨                 The Group manages its liquidity on a prudent basis with
  在符合法定準則及確保有充足之流動性                 the objective to comply with the statutory standards and
  及融資能力，以應付日常的業務營運及                 to ensure that there is an adequate liquidity and funding
  能承受嚴重資金壓力。本集團已採納香                 capacity to meet normal business operations and to
  港金管局指定之流動性維持比率（ 「流動               withstand severe liquidity stresses. The Group has adopted
  性維持比率」）及核心資金比率（「核心資               the Liquidity Maintenance Ratio (“LMR”) and the Core Funding
  金比率」
     ）為呈報本集團流動資金狀況之                 Ratio (“CFR”) as the regulatory standards specified by the

       （丑）金融資產及負債之公平值                      (l)   Fair values of financial assets and liabilities

        公平值受限於須由董事會負責確保本集                  Fair values are subject to a control framework that the
        團有適當的估值管治及控制程序之控制                  Board is held responsible for ensuring proper valuation
        框架。董事會授權財資及投資風險委員                  governance and control processes of the Group. It delegates

        險管理及監控部和獨立專業合資格估值                  financial instruments to the TIRC. Valuation is performed

        程之完整性。                             actuaries and the valuation results are periodically verified to
                                           ensure the integrity of the fair value measurement process.

        金融工具之公平值乃在目前市場情況下                  The fair value of financial instruments is the price that would
        市場參與者於計量日進行之有序交易中                  be received to sell an asset or paid to transfer a liability
        出售資產所收取或轉移負債所支付之價                  in an orderly transaction between market participants at
        格，不論該價格為直接可觀察或使用估                  the measurement date under current market conditions
        值方法估計。                             regardless of whether that price is directly observable or
                                           estimated using a valuation technique.

        凡金融工具之報價隨時且定期由交易                   A financial instrument is regarded as quoted in an active
        所、交易商、經紀人、行業組織、定價服                 market if quoted prices are readily and regularly available
        務及監管機構發佈，則被視作為活躍市                  from an exchange, dealer, broker, industry group, pricing
        場報價之金融工具。於活躍市場之報價                  service or regulatory agency. A quoted price in an active
        為公平值提供最可靠之證據，並須於可                  market provides the most reliable evidence of fair value and
        獲得時使用。倘金融資產或金融負債有                  shall be used whenever available. If a financial asset or a
        買入價及賣出價，本集團將採用買賣差                  financial liability has a bid price and an ask price, the price
        價中在該等情況下最能代表公平值之                   within the bid-ask spread that is most representative of fair
        價格。                                value in the circumstances is used by the Group.

        倘金融工具之可觀察市場報價未能直接                  Where observable market quotation of financial instruments
        獲得，本集團利用合適及獲廣泛認可之                  is not directly available, the Group estimates the fair value
        估值方法估計該等金融工具之公平值，                  of such financial instruments by using appropriate valuation
        包括現值方法及標準期權定價模型。於                  techniques that are widely recognised including present value
        應用該等金融工具之估值方法時，本集                  techniques and standard option pricing models. In applying
        團盡最大限度使用相關可觀察依據（例                  valuation techniques for these financial instruments, the
        如：利率、匯率、波動性、信貸息差） ，而               Group maximises the use of relevant observable inputs (for
        盡最少限度使用不可觀察依據。例如利                  examples, interest rates, foreign exchange rates, volatilities,
        率掉期合約之公平值按估計的未來現金                  credit spreads) and minimises the use of unobservable inputs.
        流之現值計算，遠期外匯合約之公平值                  For example, the fair value of interest-rate swaps is calculated
        一般根據現行遠期匯率計算，而期權合                  as the present value of the estimated future cash flows, the
        約之公平值則按合適之定價模型計算，                  fair value of foreign exchange forward contracts is generally
        如Black-Scholes模型。                  based on current forward rates and the fair value of option
                                           contracts is derived using appropriate pricing models, such as

     本集團的保險業務涉及多種風險，包括保險              The Groupʼs insurance business is exposed to multiple risks,
     風險、產品風險、投資風險及業務風險。本集             including insurance risk, product risk, investment risk and business
     團相信有效的風險管理是控制及經營保險業              risks. We believe that effective risk management is an integral part
     務的關鍵，有助維持本集團業務的盈利能力              of our insurance businessʼ control process and operations, and that
     和穩健。                             effective control of risks assists to maintain the profitability and
                                      stability of our business.

     保險業務的主要風險及相關的控制程序                The key risks of our insurance business and related risk control
     如下：                              process are as follows:

       （甲）保險風險                             (a) Insurance risk

        本集團的保險業務是承保有關保險的風                  The Groupʼs insurance operation is in the business of
        險，而所承保之各類別或事件的風險，視                 underwriting insurance risk and retains various maximum
        乎風險的種類均設有最高保額，超額的                  amounts per risk or event dependent on the type of risk with
        風險將按不時檢討之各種轉保及相關協                  the excess being reinsured through various reinsurance and
        議分保。另外，保險集團亦採取分保作災                 related agreements which are regularly reviewed. Catastrophe
        難補償安排以減低因特定事件索償（可                  cover arrangements are also in place whereby a number
        能涉及多項索償）對本集團的風險。                   of claims relating to a specific incident in aggregate would
                                           represent a material risk to the Group are reinsured.

        承保及索償方法及程序均需記錄及                    Underwriting and claims practices and procedures are
        檢討。                                documented and reviewed.

       （乙）產品風險                             (b) Product risk

        及如有需要交由內部及外部的獨立精算                  being reviewed and where appropriate assessed by internal
        師評核。                               and external independent actuaries.

       （丙）投資風險                             (c) Investment risk
        保險集團的投資方式是維持盡量平衡保                  Our investment practice is to maintain a conservatively
        險業務資產與對保單持有人的負債之回                  invested portfolio which attempts to maintain value whilst
        報、年期及貨幣的配合，並以保守投資組                 matching assets and policyholder liabilities as appropriate, by
        合盡力保持投資價值，當中考慮的因素                  yield, duration and currency taking account of the associated
        包括相關的風險、稅務及監管規定。                   risks, taxation and regulatory requirements.

  場之保險償付能力法規監管，且已遵守                 to insurance solvency regulations in the markets in which
  當地之償付能力法規。本集團已於其風                 they issue insurance and investment contracts, and where
  險管理架構內嵌入合適之測試，以確保                 they have to comply with the local solvency regulations. The
  持續及完全遵守有關法規。各市場內之                 Group has embedded in its risk management framework the
  各保險公司之償付能力要求須遵照當地                 necessary tests to ensure continuous and full compliance with
  規定，而各司法管轄區之規定可能相異。                such regulations. The solvency requirement of each insurance
                                    company in each market is subject to local requirements,
                                    which may differ from jurisdiction to jurisdiction.

丙. 內部審核處的角色                    C.   The role of Internal Audit

  本集團之內部審核處是一個獨立、客觀            The Groupʼs Internal Audit Division is an independent, objective
  及顧問性質的部門，集中於改進和維持            assurance and consulting unit, which is designed to focus on
  本集團業務及後勤部門良好的內部控             enhancing and sustaining sound internal control in all business
  制。該處向一獨立非執行董事所主持的            and operational units of the Group. The Division reports
  集團審核委員會作出功能上的匯報。內            functionally to the Group AC, which is chaired by an Independent
  部審核處處理各類不同形式的內部控制            Non-Executive Director. The Division conducts a wide variety
  活動，例如合規性審計，操作和系統覆查           of internal control activities such as compliance audits and
  以確保本集團控制系統的完整性、效率            operations and systems reviews to ensure the integrity, efficiency
  和有效性。                        and effectiveness of the systems of control of the Group.

股息政策                                   DIVIDEND POLICY
本公司之股息政策旨在維持穩定持續派付                     The Companyʼs dividend policy aims to pay sustainable dividends
股息。本公司在釐定股息分派時，考慮因素                    over time. In determining dividend distribution, the Company
包括業務一般狀況、財務業績表現、業務增                    takes into account factors such as general business conditions,
長前景、資本要求、適用於本公司業務的監                    financial results, business growth prospects, capital requirements,
管規定、股東權益及董事會認為相關的任何                    regulatory requirements applicable to the Companyʼs businesses,
其他因素。                                  shareholdersʼ interests and any other factors the Directors consider
                                       to be relevant.

購買、出售或贖回證券                             PURCHASE, SALE OR REDEMPTION OF SECURITIES
截 至2024年6月30日 止6個 月 期 間，除 大 新          There was no purchase, sale or redemption by the Company, or any
